        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260406_duplicate_as_new_generation.sql\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.duplicate_as_new_generation(\n+  p_shot_id uuid,\n+  p_generation_id uuid,\n+  p_project_id uuid,\n+  p_timeline_frame integer,\n+  p_next_timeline_frame integer DEFAULT NULL,\n+  p_target_timeline_frame integer DEFAULT NULL\n+)\n+RETURNS jsonb\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql\n+AS $function$\n+DECLARE\n+  v_shot_id uuid;\n+  v_source_location text;\n+  v_source_thumbnail_url text;\n+  v_source_params jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;\n+  v_new_params jsonb;\n+  v_new_generation_id uuid;\n+  v_new_shot_generation_id uuid;\n+  v_target_timeline_frame integer;\n+  v_new_timeline_frame integer;\n+  v_created_at timestamptz;\n+  v_generation_type text;\n+  v_offset integer := 1;\n+BEGIN\n+  IF auth.role() <> 'service_role' AND NOT EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1\n+    FROM public.projects p\n+    WHERE p.id = p_project_id\n+      AND p.user_id = auth.uid()\n+  ) THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized to duplicate generations in this project';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT s.id\n+  INTO v_shot_id\n+  FROM public.shots s\n+  WHERE s.id = p_shot_id\n+    AND s.project_id = p_project_id;\n+\n+  IF v_shot_id IS NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Shot % was not found in project %', p_shot_id, p_project_id;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT\n+    COALESCE(gv.location, g.location),\n+    COALESCE(gv.thumbnail_url, g.thumbnail_url),\n+    COALESCE(gv.params, g.params, '{}'::jsonb)\n+  INTO\n+    v_source_location,\n+    v_source_thumbnail_url,\n+    v_source_params\n+  FROM public.generations g\n+  LEFT JOIN public.generation_variants gv ON gv.id = g.primary_variant_id\n+  WHERE g.id = p_generation_id\n+    AND g.project_id = p_project_id;\n+\n+  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Generation % was not found in project %', p_generation_id, p_project_id;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF v_source_location IS NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Generation % is missing location', p_generation_id;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('duplicate_as_new_generation'), hashtext(p_shot_id::text));\n+\n+  v_generation_type := CASE\n+    WHEN v_source_location ~* '\\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\\?.*)?$' THEN 'video'\n+    ELSE 'image'\n+  END;\n+\n+  v_new_params := v_source_params\n+    || jsonb_build_object(\n+      'source', 'timeline_duplicate',\n+      'source_generation_id', p_generation_id,\n+      'duplicated_at', statement_timestamp()\n+    );\n+\n+  INSERT INTO public.generations (\n+    project_id,\n+    type,\n+    based_on,\n+    params\n+  )\n+  VALUES (\n+    p_project_id,\n+    v_generation_type,\n+    p_generation_id,\n+    v_new_params\n+  )\n+  RETURNING id, created_at\n+  INTO v_new_generation_id, v_created_at;\n+\n+  INSERT INTO public.generation_variants (\n+    generation_id,\n+    location,\n+    thumbnail_url,\n+    is_primary,\n+    variant_type,\n+    name,\n+    params\n+  )\n+  VALUES (\n+    v_new_generation_id,\n+    v_source_location,\n+    v_source_thumbnail_url,\n+    TRUE,\n+    'original',\n+    'Original',\n+    v_new_params\n+  );\n+\n+  v_target_timeline_frame := CASE\n+    WHEN p_target_timeline_frame IS NOT NULL THEN p_target_timeline_frame\n+    WHEN p_next_timeline_frame IS NOT NULL THEN floor((p_timeline_frame + p_next_timeline_frame) / 2.0)::integer\n+    ELSE p_timeline_frame + 30\n+  END;\n+\n+  v_new_timeline_frame := GREATEST(0, round(v_target_timeline_frame::numeric)::integer);\n+\n+  IF EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1\n+    FROM public.shot_generations sg\n+    WHERE sg.shot_id = p_shot_id\n+      AND sg.timeline_frame = v_new_timeline_frame\n+  ) THEN\n+    WHILE v_offset < 1000 LOOP\n+      IF NOT EXISTS (\n+        SELECT 1\n+        FROM public.shot_generations sg\n+        WHERE sg.shot_id = p_shot_id\n+          AND sg.timeline_frame = v_new_timeline_frame + v_offset\n+      ) THEN\n+        v_new_timeline_frame := v_new_timeline_frame + v_offset;\n+        EXIT;\n+      END IF;\n+\n+      IF v_new_timeline_frame - v_offset >= 0 AND NOT EXISTS (\n+        SELECT 1\n+        FROM public.shot_generations sg\n+        WHERE sg.shot_id = p_shot_id\n+          AND sg.timeline_frame = v_new_timeline_frame - v_offset\n+      ) THEN\n+        v_new_timeline_frame := v_new_timeline_frame - v_offset;\n+        EXIT;\n+      END IF;\n+\n+      v_offset := v_offset + 1;\n+    END LOOP;\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  INSERT INTO public.shot_generations (\n+    shot_id,\n+    generation_id,\n+    timeline_frame\n+  )\n+  VALUES (\n+    p_shot_id,\n+    v_new_generation_id,\n+    v_new_timeline_frame\n+  )\n+  RETURNING id\n+  INTO v_new_shot_generation_id;\n+\n+  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\n+    'new_generation_id', v_new_generation_id,\n+    'new_shot_generation_id', v_new_shot_generation_id,\n+    'timeline_frame', v_new_timeline_frame,\n+    'location', v_source_location,\n+    'thumbnail_url', COALESCE(v_source_thumbnail_url, v_source_location),\n+    'type', v_generation_type,\n+    'params', v_new_params,\n+    'created_at', v_created_at\n+  );\n+END;\n+$function$;\n+\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.duplicate_as_new_generation(uuid, uuid, uuid, integer, integer, integer) TO authenticated;\n+\n+COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.duplicate_as_new_generation(uuid, uuid, uuid, integer, integer, integer) IS\n+'Duplicates a generation into the same shot atomically, creates a new primary variant, resolves timeline collisions in-function, and returns the hydrated data needed for direct cache updates.';\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: checked the two user-visible `must` goals for the selection ring and locked-pane chat. they describe the right product outcomes, but they are mostly manual ui behaviors rather than code-review-verifiable gates, so they are not fully testable from the listed automated checks alone. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: checked the revised `must` criterion about eliminating duplicated server-side helpers. as written, the plan could satisfy 'all import from utils.ts' for `generation.ts`, `create-task.ts`, and `messages.ts` and still break four existing `messages.ts` consumers, so the criterion is incomplete unless it also requires preserving or updating the `messages.ts` export surface. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-param-schema-evolution: clips with stale or partial params from a previous schema version will not be automatically normalized/backfilled when the effect schema changes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: if all 3 conflict retries fail, the fallback path risks discarding dirty local edits unless the executor wires it into the existing conflict dialog. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 3 of 5.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T5', 'T7', 'T8', 'T9']

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.

        Idea:
Migrate all remaining shot creation flows to use atomic RPCs and direct cache updates, eliminating the split-ownership pattern where mutations create partial cache entries and invalidation fills them in later. The create_shot_with_generations RPC and upsertShotInCache pattern from the previous megaplan (fix-the-fundamental-cache-20260406-0236) is the template. Flows to migrate: (1) createShotWithGenerationPath (singular) - uses create_shot_with_image RPC + applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate with partial optimistic data. Should use create_shot_with_generations RPC (N=1 case) instead. (2) createShotWithFilesPath - creates empty shot then uploads N files each triggering separate addImageToShot with own optimistic+invalidation. Needs post-upload upsertShotInCache with complete data. (3) useDuplicateAsNewGeneration - 4 sequential DB writes, no optimistic update, 5 separate invalidations. Needs atomic RPC. After migration, clean up: (4) Remove applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate (shotCacheUpdate.ts) - no longer needed once all paths use real data. (5) Remove setTimeout/delayed invalidation hacks. (6) Audit all enqueueGenerationsInvalidation callers - remove includeShots:true where cache is updated directly. (7) Remove the deprecated createShot field from CreateShotWithGenerationsPathInput. Be thorough - search for insertAutoPositionedShotGeneration, applyAtomicShotCacheUpdate, includeShots, setTimeout.*invalidat to find all instances.
